The only visible writing is the exact English copy ‘WE HOLD’ and ‘CAN I GO?’; both phrases are large, correctly spelled, complete, horizontal, unobstructed, and clearly legible, with no Chinese characters, pinyin, bilingual text, gibberish, random letters, numbers, logos, fine print, or watermark.
